**Total services:** 127
**Total methods:** 805

This document was generated from `core.get_methods` on the live TrueNAS instance.
The full raw JSON is available alongside this file as `truenas-api-methods.json`.

## Notes

- Method parameters ("knobs") are summarised from the `accepts` JSON Schema.
- `?` denotes optional parameters; `=` shows defaults where declared.
- `roles` lists the TrueNAS role groups allowed to call the method.
- `job: true` indicates the method returns a long-running job object.
